## `AFG-4719-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Azo-Dyes-Safety Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Retail Concession Product Quality Certification & Compliance
- **Intro:** Azo-Dyes-Safety Metrologist — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Retail Concession Product Quality Certification & Compliance — Verify Calibration Status and Reference Standards: Calibration certificate validity ≤ 12 months; reference standard expiry date not exceeded; drift ≤ ±5% of initial absorbance at…
